Mystery Face card images: credits and licences (generated by tools/deck-credits.py)
Real people and the Cartoons & Classics cards: free-licence images from Wikimedia Commons.
Movie characters: the studios' own character images (copyright their owners), used for fan play;
each line names the studio and the wiki page the image came from.
Each line: name: author or studio, licence (link), source page.
Cropped and resized to 256x256 WebP; no other changes. Mixed-deck cards are copies of the cards below.
